4. N → B → E → F → X → K → G (edges: (N,B), (B,E), (E,F), (F,X), (X,K), (K,G))

Next, analyze which edges are critical. Paths 1-3 rely on the edge (H,G) to reach G, while Path 4 relies on (K,G). - Path 1: (H,G) is removed, so broken.
- Path 2: (H,G) is removed, so broken.
- Path 3: (H,G) is removed, so broken.
- Path 4: (K,G) is removed, so broken.
